# Attributions

Studio's library content is either made for Studio or openly licensed. This file lists every third-party source and licence across the core bundle and every optional pack; each pack also ships its own `LICENSES.md`. The in-app Licences screen shows the same information.

## Packs

- **Studio core library** (`core`): 10507 items, 291 MB — core bundle, ships with the app. Ships inside the app: stickers, emoji, shapes, text art, frames, green-screen stills and clips, overlays, backgrounds, sounds and a set of fonts.
- **Fluent emoji stickers** (`fluent-stickers`): 1595 items, 38 MB — optional pack. Microsoft's Fluent Emoji in the 3D style, MIT licensed.
- **More fonts** (`fonts-google`): 2018 items, 2257 MB — optional pack. The whole Google Fonts collection (OFL and Apache-2.0 families).
- **More backgrounds** (`more-backgrounds`): 32 items, 12 MB — optional pack. The rest of our background sets, beyond what fits in the core bundle.
- **More green-screen items** (`more-green-screen`): 40 items, 25 MB — optional pack. The rest of our green screen sets, beyond what fits in the core bundle.
- **More motion clips** (`more-motion`): 20 items, 65 MB — optional pack. The rest of our motion sets, beyond what fits in the core bundle.
- **More photos** (`more-photos`): 96 items, 33 MB — optional pack. The rest of our photo sets, beyond what fits in the core bundle.
- **More stickers** (`more-stickers`): 1379 items, 223 MB — optional pack. The rest of our sticker sets, beyond what fits in the core bundle.
- **Music by Kevin MacLeod** (`music-incompetech`): 380 items, 2167 MB — optional pack. Royalty-free music by Kevin MacLeod (incompetech.com), CC BY 4.0: the credit line must be shown wherever a track is used.
- **Music from OpenGameArt** (`music-opengameart`): 91 items, 262 MB — optional pack. Tracks from OpenGameArt.org, CC0 and CC BY 4.0.
- **OpenMoji emoji (ShareAlike set)** (`openmoji`): 4486 items, 13 MB — optional pack, ShareAlike set. The OpenMoji emoji set, CC BY-SA 4.0. Kept in its own pack because of the ShareAlike condition.
- **More sound effects** (`sound-effects-opengameart`): 2566 items, 721 MB — optional pack. Sound effects from OpenGameArt.org, CC0.

## Made for Studio (Studio)

<https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0>

These items were made for Studio: generated with Midjourney under the owner's paid plan, then selected, named, cut out, keyed and resized by the Studio team. They are distributed with Studio under the Apache License 2.0 like the rest of the project.

### Made for Studio

2546 items: sticker, emoji, text art, frame, overlay, background, photo, green screen, motion. Modified for Studio (resized, cut out, keyed or re-encoded).

- Made for Studio
